data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport { GameState, ResultStats, TypingWord } from './types';\nimport { WORD_LIST } from './wordData';\nimport TypingDisplay from './components/TypingDisplay';\nimport ResultModal from './components/ResultModal';\nimport './index.css';\n\nconst TOTAL_TIME = 60;\n\nconst calculateRank = (correctChars: number, accuracy: number): string => {\n  if (accuracy < 70) return '慎重派タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 380) return '神速のタイピングマスター';\n  if (correctChars >= 300) return '超一流タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 220) return '一流タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 150) return '一人前タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 80) return '見習いタイパー';\n  return '初心者タイパー';\n};\n\nconst shuffleArray = (array: TypingWord[]): TypingWord[] => {\n  const shuffled = [...array];\n  for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n    [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];\n  }\n  return shuffled;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [words, setWords] = useState<TypingWord[]>(() => shuffleArray(WORD_LIST));\n  const [gameState, setGameState] = useState<GameState>({\n    status: 'idle',\n    timeLeft: TOTAL_TIME,\n    totalCharsTyped: 0,\n    correctChars: 0,\n    missCount: 0,\n    combo: 0,\n    maxCombo: 0,\n    currentWordIndex: 0,\n    currentCharIndex: 0\n  });\n\n  const [isMiss, setIsMiss] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [resultStats, setResultStats] = useState<ResultStats | null>(null);\n\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  const missTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  const currentWord: TypingWord | undefined = words[gameState.currentWordIndex];\n  const nextWord: TypingWord | undefined = words[(gameState.currentWordIndex + 1) % words.length];\n\n  const focusInput = useCallback(() => {\n    if (inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.focus();\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const finishGame = useCallback(() => {\n    setGameState((prev) => {\n      const accuracy = prev.totalCharsTyped > 0\n        ? (prev.correctChars / prev.totalCharsTyped) * 100\n        : 0;\n      const cpm = Math.round(prev.correctChars);\n      const rank = calculateRank(prev.correctChars, accuracy);\n\n      setResultStats({\n        totalChars: prev.totalCharsTyped,\n        correctChars: prev.correctChars,\n        missCount: prev.missCount,\n        accuracy,\n        cpm,\n        rank\n      });\n\n      return {\n        ...prev,\n        status: 'finished',\n        timeLeft: 0\n      };\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const startGame = useCallback(() => {\n    const newShuffled = shuffleArray(WORD_LIST);\n    setWords(newShuffled);\n    setGameState({\n      status: 'playing',\n      timeLeft: TOTAL_TIME,\n      totalCharsTyped: 0,\n      correctChars: 0,\n      missCount: 0,\n      combo: 0,\n      maxCombo: 0,\n      currentWordIndex: 0,\n      currentCharIndex: 0\n    });\n    setResultStats(null);\n    setIsMiss(false);\n\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      focusInput();\n    }, 50);\n  }, [focusInput]);\n\n  // タイマー管理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (gameState.status !== 'playing') return;\n\n    const timer = setInterval(() => {\n      setGameState((prev) => {\n        if (prev.timeLeft <= 1) {\n          clearInterval(timer);\n          finishGame();\n          return { ...prev, timeLeft: 0 };\n        }\n        return { ...prev, timeLeft: prev.timeLeft - 1 };\n      });\n    }, 1000);\n\n    return () => clearInterval(timer);\n  }, [gameState.status, finishGame]);\n\n  // キー入力処理\n  const handleKeyProcess = useCallback((pressedChar: string) => {\n    if (gameState.status !== 'playing' || !currentWord) return;\n\n    const expectedChar = currentWord.romaji[gameState.currentCharIndex];\n\n    if (pressedChar.toLowerCase() === expectedChar.toLowerCase()) {\n      const nextCharIndex = gameState.currentCharIndex + 1;\n      const isWordCompleted = nextCharIndex >= currentWord.romaji.length;\n\n      setGameState((prev) => {\n        const nextCombo = prev.combo + 1;\n        const nextMaxCombo = Math.max(prev.maxCombo, nextCombo);\n        const nextWordIndex = isWordCompleted\n          ? (prev.currentWordIndex + 1) % words.length\n          : prev.currentWordIndex;\n\n        return {\n          ...prev,\n          totalCharsTyped: prev.totalCharsTyped + 1,\n          correctChars: prev.correctChars + 1,\n          combo: nextCombo,\n          maxCombo: nextMaxCombo,\n          currentWordIndex: nextWordIndex,\n          currentCharIndex: isWordCompleted ? 0 : nextCharIndex\n        };\n      });\n    } else {\n      if (missTimerRef.current) {\n        clearTimeout(missTimerRef.current);\n      }\n      setIsMiss(true);\n      missTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        setIsMiss(false);\n      }, 300);\n\n      setGameState((prev) => ({\n        ...prev,\n        totalCharsTyped: prev.totalCharsTyped + 1,\n        missCount: prev.missCount + 1,\n        combo: 0\n      }));\n    }\n  }, [gameState.status, gameState.currentCharIndex, currentWord, words.length]);\n\n  // キーボードイベントハンドラ\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (gameState.status !== 'playing') {\n        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') {\n          if (gameState.status === 'idle') {\n            e.preventDefault();\n            startGame();\n          }\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.key === 'Tab' || e.key === 'Escape') return;\n\n      if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleKeyProcess(e.key);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [gameState.status, handleKeyProcess, startGame]);\n\n  // モバイル用Inputの入力検知\n  const handleMobileInput = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    const value = e.target.value;\n    if (value.length > 0) {\n      const lastChar = value.slice(-1);\n      handleKeyProcess(lastChar);\n      e.target.value = '';\n    }\n  };\n\n  const nextChar = currentWord ? currentWord.romaji[gameState.currentCharIndex] : '';\n  const gaugePercent = (gameState.timeLeft / TOTAL_TIME) * 100;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\" onClick={focusInput}>\n      <header className=\"header-panel\">\n        <h1 className=\"title-text\">タイピングマスター 60</h1>\n        <p className=\"subtitle-text\">60秒で限界に挑むローマ字タイピング</p>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className=\"status-bar\">\n        <div className=\"time-badge\">\n          <span>残り時間</span>\n          <span>{gameState.timeLeft} 秒</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"time-gauge-bg\">\n          <div\n            className=\"time-gauge-fill\"\n            style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, gaugePercent)}%` }}\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>\n          <div className=\"score-pill\">\n            <span>打鍵数:</span>\n            <strong>{gameState.correctChars}</strong>\n          </div>\n          {gameState.combo > 2 && (\n            <div className=\"combo-badge\">\n              <span>{gameState.combo} COMBO!</span>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      <main className=\"game-area\">\n        {/* モバイルおよびフォーカス用不可視インプット */}\n        <input\n          ref={inputRef}\n          type=\"text\"\n          className=\"hidden-input\"\n          onChange={handleMobileInput}\n          autoComplete=\"off\"\n          autoCorrect=\"off\"\n          autoCapitalize=\"off\"\n          spellCheck={false}\n          aria-label=\"タイピング入力\"\n        />\n\n        {gameState.status === 'idle' && (\n          <div className=\"start-overlay\">\n            <button\n i > 0; i--) {\n    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n    [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];\n  }\n  return shuffled;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [words, setWords] = useState<TypingWord[]>(() => shuffleArray(WORD_LIST));\n  const [gameState, setGameState] = useState<GameState>({\n    status: 'idle',\n    timeLeft: TOTAL_TIME,\n    totalCharsTyped: 0,\n    correctChars: 0,\n    missCount: 0,\n    combo: 0,\n    maxCombo: 0,\n    currentWordIndex: 0,\n    currentCharIndex: 0\n  });\n\n  const [isMiss, setIsMiss] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [resultStats, setResultStats] = useState<ResultStats | null>(null);\n\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  const missTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  // 打鍵ごとのリスナー再登録を防止するため最新の状態をRefで保持\n  const stateRef = useRef({ gameState, words });\n  useEffect(() => {\n    stateRef.current = { gameState, words };\n  }, [gameState, words]);\n\n  const currentWord: TypingWord | undefined = words[gameState.currentWordIndex];\n  const nextWord: TypingWord | undefined = words[(gameState.currentWordIndex + 1) % words.length];\n\n  const focusInput = useCallback(() => {\n    if (inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.focus();\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const finishGame = useCallback(() => {\n    setGameState((prev) => {\n      const accuracy = prev.totalCharsTyped > 0\n        ? (prev.correctChars / prev.totalCharsTyped) * 100\n        : 0;\n      const cpm = Math.round(prev.correctChars);\n      const rank = calculateRank(prev.correctChars, accuracy);\n\n      setResultStats({\n        totalChars: prev.totalCharsTyped,\n        correctChars: prev.correctChars,\n        missCount: prev.missCount,\n        accuracy,\n        cpm,\n        rank,\n        maxCombo: prev.maxCombo\n      });\n\n      return {\n        ...prev,\n        status: 'finished',\n        timeLeft: 0\n      };\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const startGame = useCallback(() => {\n    const newShuffled = shuffleArray(WORD_LIST);\n    setWords(newShuffled);\n    setGameState({\n      status: 'playing',\n      timeLeft: TOTAL_TIME,\n      totalCharsTyped: 0,\n      correctChars: 0,\n      missCount: 0,\n      combo: 0,\n      maxCombo: 0,\n      currentWordIndex: 0,\n      currentCharIndex: 0\n    });\n    setResultStats(null);\n    setIsMiss(false);\n\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      focusInput();\n    }, 50);\n  }, [focusInput]);\n\n  // タイマー管理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (gameState.status !== 'playing') return;\n\n    const timer = setInterval(() => {\n      setGameState((prev) => {\n        if (prev.timeLeft <= 1) {\n          clearInterval(timer);\n          finishGame();\n          return { ...prev, timeLeft: 0 };\n        }\n        return { ...prev, timeLeft: prev.timeLeft - 1 };\n      });\n    }, 1000);\n\n    return () => clearInterval(timer);\n  }, [gameState.status, finishGame]);\n\n  // キー入力処理\n  const handleKeyProcess = useCallback((pressedChar: string) => {\n    const { gameState: currentGameState, words: currentWords } = stateRef.current;\n    if (currentGameState.status !== 'playing') return;\n\n    const targetWord = currentWords[currentGameState.currentWordIndex];\n    if (!targetWord) return;\n\n    const expectedChar = targetWord.romaji[currentGameState.currentCharIndex];\n\n    if (pressedChar.toLowerCase() === expectedChar.toLowerCase()) {\n      const nextCharIndex = currentGameState.currentCharIndex + 1;\n      const isWordCompleted = nextCharIndex >= targetWord.romaji.length;\n\n      setGameState((prev) => {\n        const nextCombo = prev.combo + 1;\n        const nextMaxCombo = Math.max(prev.maxCombo, nextCombo);\n        const nextWordIndex = isWordCompleted\n          ? (prev.currentWordIndex + 1) % currentWords.length\n          : prev.currentWordIndex;\n\n        return {\n          ...prev,\n          totalCharsTyped: prev.totalCharsTyped + 1,\n          correctChars: prev.correctChars + 1,\n          combo: nextCombo,\n          maxCombo: nextMaxCombo,\n          currentWordIndex: nextWordIndex,\n          currentCharIndex: isWordCompleted ? 0 : nextCharIndex\n        };\n      });\n    } else {\n      if (missTimerRef.current) {\n        clearTimeout(missTimerRef.current);\n      }\n      setIsMiss(true);\n      missTimerRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        setIsMiss(false);\n      }, 300);\n\n      setGameState((prev) => ({\n        ...prev,\n        totalCharsTyped: prev.totalCharsTyped + 1,\n        missCount: prev.missCount + 1,\n        combo: 0\n      }));\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // キーボードイベントハンドラ（リスナーの毎打鍵再登録を防止）\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      const currentStatus = stateRef.current.gameState.status;\n\n      if (currentStatus === 'finished') {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (currentStatus !== 'playing') {\n        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') {\n          if (currentStatus === 'idle') {\n            e.preventDefault();\n            startGame();\n          }\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.key === 'Tab' || e.key === 'Escape') return;\n\n      if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleKeyProcess(e.key);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [startGame, handleKeyProcess]);\n\n  // モバイル用Inputの入力検知\n  const handleMobileInput = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    if (stateRef.current.gameState.status !== 'playing') {\n      e.target.value = '';\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const value = e.target.value;\n    if (value.length > 0) {\n      const lastChar = value.slice(-1);\n      handleKeyProcess(lastChar);\n      e.target.value = '';\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleContainerClick = () => {\n    if (gameState.status === 'playing') {\n      focusInput();\n    }\n  };\n\n  const nextChar = currentWord ? currentWord.romaji[gameState.currentCharIndex] : '';\n  const gaugePercent = (gameState.timeLeft / TOTAL_TIME) * 100;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\" onClick={handleContainerClick}>\n      <header className=\"header-panel\">\n        <h1 className=\"title-text\">タイピングマスター 60</h1>\n        <p className=\"subtitle-text\">60秒で限界に挑むローマ字タイピング</p>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className=\"status-bar\">\n        <div className=\"time-badge\">\n          <span>残り時間</span>\n          <span>{gameState.timeLeft} 秒</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"time-gauge-bg\">\n          <div\n            className=\"time-gauge-fill\"\n            style={{ width: `${Math.max(0, gaugePercent)}%` }}\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', onChange={handleMobileInput}\n          disabled={gameState.status === 'finished'}\n          autoComplete=\"off\"\n          autoCorrect=\"off\"\n          autoCapitalize=\"off\"\n          spellCheck={false}\n          aria-label=\"タイピング入力\"\n        />\n\n        {gameState.status === 'idle' && (\n          <div className=\"start-overlay\">\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              className=\"start-btn\"\n              onClick={startGame}\n            >\n              スタート (Space)\n            </button>\n            <p className=\"instruction-text\">\n              スペースキーまたは画面タップでタイピング開始\n            </p>\n          </div>\n        )}\n\n        {currentWord && (\n          <>\n            <TypingDisplay\n         <span>次の問題:</span>\n                <span>{nextWord.kanji}</span>\n              </div>\n            )}\n          </>\n        )}\n      </main>\n\n      <p className=\"instruction-text\">\n        ※ 入力が反応しない場合は、画面をタップしてキーボードをフォーカスしてください\n      </p>\n\n      {gameState.status === 'finished' && resultStats && (\n        <ResultModal stats={resultStats} onRestart={startGame} />\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

'typing-card shake-anim' : 'typing-card';\n\n  return (\n    <div className={cardClassName} aria-live=\"polite\">\n      <div className=\"hiragana-text\">{word.hiragana}</div>\n      <div className=\"kanji-text\">{word.kanji}</div>\n      <div className=\"romaji-container\">\n        {romajiChars.map((char, index) => {\n          let charClass = 'romaji-char char-remaining';\n          const isCurrent = index === charIndex;\n\n          if (index < charIndex) {\n            charClass = 'romaji-char char-typed';\n          } else if (isCurrent) {\n            charClass = 'romaji-char char-current';\n          }\n\n          return (\n            <span\n              key={`${word.id ?? stats, onRestart }: ResultModalProps) => {\n  const restartButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    restartButtonRef.current?.focus();\n  }, []);\n\n  const maxCombo = (stats as unknown as { maxCombo?: number }).maxCombo ?? 0;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"modal-overlay\" role=\"dialog\" aria-modal=\"true\">\n      <div className=\"result-card\">\n        <h2         ref={restartButtonRef}\n          type=\"button\"\n          className=\"restart-btn\"\n          onClick={() => onRestart()}\n        >\n          もう一度チャレンジする\n        </button>\n ResultStats, TypingWord } from './types';\nimport { WORD_LIST } from './wordData';\nimport TypingDisplay from './components/TypingDisplay';\nimport ResultModal from './components/ResultModal';\nimport './index.css';\n\nconst TOTAL_TIME = 60;\n\nconst calculateRank = (correctChars: number, accuracy: number): string => {\n  if (accuracy < 70) return '慎重派タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 380) return '神速のタイピングマスター';\n  if (correctChars >= 300) return '超一流タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 220) return '一流タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 150) return '一人前タイパー';\n  if (correctChars >= 80) return '見習いタイパー';\n  return '初心者タイパー';\n};\n\nconst shuffleArray = (array: TypingWord[]): TypingWord[] => {\n  const shuffled = [...array];\n  for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {\n    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));\n    [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];\n  }\n  return shuffled;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [words, setWords] = useState<TypingWord[]>(() => shuffleArray(WORD_LIST));\n  const [gameState, setGameState] = useState<GameState>({\n    status: 'idle',\n    timeLeft: TOTAL_TIME,\n    totalCharsTyped: 0,\n    correctChars: 0,\n    missCount: 0,\n    combo: 0,\n    maxCombo: 0,\n    currentWordIndex: 0,\n    currentCharIndex: 0\n  });\n\n  const [isMiss, setIsMiss] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [resultStats, setResultStats] = useState<ResultStats | null>(null);\n\n  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);\n  const missTimerRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n\n  // 打鍵ごとのリスナー再登録を防止するため最新の状態をRefで保持\n  const stateRef = useRef({ gameState, words });\n  useEffect(() => {\n    stateRef.current = { gameState, words };\n  }, [gameState, words]);\n\n  const currentWord: TypingWord | undefined = words[gameState.currentWordIndex];\n  const nextWord: TypingWord | undefined = words[(gameState.currentWordIndex + 1) % words.length];\n\n  const focusInput = useCallback(() => {\n    if (inputRef.current) {\n      inputRef.current.focus();\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  const finishGame = useCallback(() => {\n    setGameState((prev) => {\n      const accuracy = prev.totalCharsTyped > 0\n        ? (prev.correctChars / prev.totalCharsTyped) * 100\n        : 0;\n      const cpm = Math.round(prev.correctChars);\n      const rank = calculateRank(prev.correctChars, accuracy);\n\n      setResultStats({\n        totalChars: prev.totalCharsTyped,\n        correctChars: prev.correctChars,\n        missCount: prev.missCount,\n        accuracy,\n        cpm,\n        rank,\n        maxCombo: prev.maxCombo\n      });\n\n      return {\n        ...prev,\n        status: 'finished',\n        timeLeft: 0\n      };\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const startGame = useCallback(() => {\n    const newShuffled = shuffleArray(WORD_LIST);\n    setWords(newShuffled);\n    setGameState({\n      status: 'playing',\n      timeLeft: TOTAL_TIME,\n      totalCharsTyped: 0,\n      correctChars: 0,\n      missCount: 0,\n      combo: 0,\n      maxCombo: 0,\n      currentWordIndex: 0,\n      currentCharIndex: 0\n    });\n    setResultStats(null);\n    setIsMiss(false);\n\n    setTimeout(() => {\n      focusInput();\n    }, 50);\n  }, [focusInput]);\n\n  // タイマー管理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (gameState.status !== 'playing') return;\n\n    const timer = setInterval(() => {\n      setGameState((prev) => {\n        if (prev.timeLeft <= 1) {\n          clearInterval(timer);\n          finishGame();\n          return { ...prev, timeLeft: 0 };\n        }\n        return { ...prev, timeLeft: prev.timeLeft - currentStatus = stateRef.current.gameState.status;\n\n      if (currentStatus === 'finished') {\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (currentStatus !== 'playing') {\n        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') {\n          if (currentStatus === 'idle') {\n            e.preventDefault();\n            startGame();\n          }\n        }\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (e.key === 'Tab' || e.key === 'Escape') return;\n\n      if (e.key.length === 1 && !e.ctrlKey && !e.metaKey && !e.altKey) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleKeyProcess(e.key);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n  }, [startGame, handleKeyProcess]);\n\n  // モバイル用Inputの入力検知\n  const handleMobileInput = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    if (stateRef.current.gameState.status !== 'playing') {\n      e.target.value = '';\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const value = e.target.value;\n    if (value.length > 0) {\n      const lastChar = value.slice(-1);\n      handleKeyProcess(lastChar);\n      e.target.value = '';\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleContainerClick = ()
